# Agents · P · part 4

948 entries, pb-explorer to perf-a11y. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pb-explorer**: Use to execute ONE exploration plan written by the /pb:explore coordinator — it edits only its slot's candidate files, keeps the same renderFn and props, styles token-only, lints, and hands back. Dispatched by /pb:explore A1d / B4d on sonnet at xhigh effort, one per plan, with no design context beyond its plan; never by /pb:orchestrate. Not for the live slice — that is pb-builder. ([danhnbui/prototype-builder](https://github.com/danhnbui/prototype-builder/blob/HEAD/pb/agents/pb-explorer.md) · ★2)
- **pb-flow**: Use to generate or update the UX Design / Flow tab — a Mermaid wireflow plus a numbered user-story test checklist with categorized scenarios. Wraps /pb:plan --flow; authors the flow slice. /pb:build reconciles it on trio writes. ([danhnbui/prototype-builder](https://github.com/danhnbui/prototype-builder/blob/HEAD/pb/agents/pb-flow.md) · ★2)
- **pb-impact-checker**: Use this agent when you need to analyze the impact of modifying a PowerBuilder object, table, or function before making changes. It traces all dependencies, inheritance chains, and cross-references to identify what could break. Use for questions like "si je modifie w_response, quel impact?", "quels objets utilisent la table salhead?". This agent runs autonomously and returns a structured impact report. ([juliendetilleux/powerbuilder-toolkit](https://github.com/juliendetilleux/powerbuilder-toolkit/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-plugin/agents/pb-impact-checker.md) · ★6)
- **pb-impact-checker**: Use this agent when you need to analyze the impact of modifying a PowerBuilder object, table, or function before making changes. It traces all dependencies, inheritance chains, and cross-references to identify what could break. Use for questions like "si je modifie w_response, quel impact?", "quels objets utilisent la table salhead?", "que se passe-t-il si je change of_save() dans uo_datawindow?". This agent runs autonomously and returns a structured impact report. ([juliendetilleux/powerbuilder-dev](https://github.com/juliendetilleux/powerbuilder-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/pb-impact-checker.md) · ★1)
- **pb-planner**: Use to break an approved spec, and the information architecture /pb:clarify chose, into an implementation plan and a per-tab task graph, where each task carries acceptance, the skill it invokes, its owning agent, its dependencies, and the registry slice it touches. Wraps /pb:plan. ([danhnbui/prototype-builder](https://github.com/danhnbui/prototype-builder/blob/HEAD/pb/agents/pb-planner.md) · ★2)
- **pb-reviewer**: Use as the acceptance/critic gate — a read-only drift audit of the trio against the constitution plus contract and secrets/PII scans. Wraps /pb:test --drift, lint_registry.py, and security_scan.py; never writes to the registry or prototype.html. ([danhnbui/prototype-builder](https://github.com/danhnbui/prototype-builder/blob/HEAD/pb/agents/pb-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **pb-reviewer**: Adversarial report-only verification for PocketBase backend claims — re-derives each claim from its cited source, re-runs its commands in its own clean room, and actively tries to refute. Use after any builder or lead report that will drive further changes. Never edits. ([hsb3/dotfiles-agents](https://github.com/hsb3/dotfiles-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/pocketbase/agents/pb-reviewer.md))
- **pb-sync-reviewer**: Reviews changes under lib/sync/, import/export, and MCP task write paths against the documented PocketBase v0.23+ gotchas plus prior Codex adversarial data-loss findings. Read-only. Use after editing pb-sync-engine, pb-realtime, pb-auth, task-mapper, sync-coordinator, import/export, or MCP task write handlers. ([vscarpenter/gsd-task-manager](https://github.com/vscarpenter/gsd-task-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pb-sync-reviewer.md) · ★41)
- **pb-tester**: Use to run the prototype's functional, server, and role/auth-enforcement tests against the rendered sandbox and record per-scenario results, or to answer a slice of a /pb:test plan as yes/no/blocked rows with evidence. Wraps /pb:test via test_run.py; read-only on the registry except for writing test results. Dispatched on sonnet with no design context, so its verdicts are independent of whoever authored the design. ([danhnbui/prototype-builder](https://github.com/danhnbui/prototype-builder/blob/HEAD/pb/agents/pb-tester.md) · ★2)
- **PBI Orchestrator**: This agent is a dynamic planner and orchestrator for Power BI workflows. It interprets the user's intent, builds a tailored execution plan from available specialist agents (connect, document, health check, ingestion assessment, DAX optimization, batch documentation), and delegates work step-by-step. Use for: documenting models, running health checks, assessing data ingestion, optimizing DAX measures, batch documentation audits across a workspace, or any combination. ([GMario83/007-Agentic-Approach](https://github.com/GMario83/007-Agentic-Approach/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/PBI%20Orchestrator.agent.md))
- **PBI Performance Advisor**: Diagnoses Power BI and Fabric semantic model performance issues — DAX Formula Engine bottlenecks, VertiPaq memory footprint, Direct Lake fallback causes, and report-level query fan-out. Read references/performance-optimization.md for full diagnostic criteria. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User reports a slow Power BI report user: "My Power BI report is really slow to load" assistant: "I'll use the pbi-performance-advisor agent to diagnose the DAX and model performance issues." &lt;commentary&gt; Slow report is the primary trigger for this performance diagnostic agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;examp… ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/powerbi-fabric/agents/pbi-performance-advisor.md) · ★19)

- **pbi-creator**: Create Azure DevOps PBIs from an approved feature plan for the Android Auth project. ([AzureAD/android-complete](https://github.com/AzureAD/android-complete/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/pbi-creator.agent.md) · ★5)
- **pbi-creator**: Create Azure DevOps work items from an approved feature plan. ([AzureAD/android-complete](https://github.com/AzureAD/android-complete/blob/HEAD/feature-orchestrator-plugin/agents/pbi-creator.agent.md) · ★5)
- **pbi-dax**: DAX specialist for the Power BI Desktop project currently open. Writes, validates, tests and optimizes measures (and calculation groups on request) through the powerbi-modeling MCP server, and runs read-only DAX queries to explore data or check numbers. Does NOT change tables/relationships (pbi-modeler), RLS (pbi-security) or report files (pbi-report). ([CPobs/PBI-assistance](https://github.com/CPobs/PBI-assistance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pbi-dax.md) · ★1)
- **pbi-designer**: Authors a PBI working design document defining component responsibilities, business logic, and interfaces. Selects third-party libraries via mandatory web search (proven track record + use-case fit) and records only web-verified library specs into the S-070 catalog type to prevent API-misuse defects. Reads existing catalog specs as read-only references; emits catalog-spec update deltas as a side-effect (lock-serialized). Writes the primary design artifact to .scrum/pbi/&lt;pbi-id&gt;/design/design.md. ([sohei56/maul-team](https://github.com/sohei56/maul-team/blob/HEAD/agents/pbi-designer.md) · ★17)
- **pbi-implementer**: Implements PBI source code per the working design doc. Writes only implementation files (test paths blocked by hook). Does not modify design docs. ([sohei56/maul-team](https://github.com/sohei56/maul-team/blob/HEAD/agents/pbi-implementer.md) · ★17)
- **pbi-knowledge-curator**: Thủ thư tri thức của Knowledge OS (agent-data-studio). Dùng khi kết thúc dự án Power BI / dữ liệu (quy trình pbi-done, pbi-pack) hoặc định kỳ: đọc projects/ trong trạm dữ liệu, rút bài học TÁI DÙNG, đóng gói vào knowledge/ theo 4 trục (tech-stack, industry, business-domain, powerbi), dedup, cập nhật INDEX + TIMELINE. KHÔNG dùng để làm việc trực tiếp với Power BI (đó là việc của các skill pbi-model / pbi-build). ([ducnguyen221/agent-data-studio](https://github.com/ducnguyen221/agent-data-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/pbi-knowledge-curator.md) · ★3)
- **pbi-manager**: Seleciona PBIs com menor impacto e menor colisão para execução paralela segura, ou decide executar uma única PBI quando houver risco alto. ([john182/poc-open-spec](https://github.com/john182/poc-open-spec/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/pbi-manager.md) · 2 copies)
- **pbi-migration-validator**: Read-only reviewer that critiques a built Power BI report against its Tableau source, figure-by-figure and as a whole dashboard, on both visual and numeric fidelity. Reports discrepancies back to the orchestrator for routing to pbi-semantic-builder/pbi-report-builder - never edits TMDL/PBIR files itself. - **pbi-modeler**: Semantic-model structure specialist for the Power BI Desktop project currently open. Creates and edits tables (from Power Query M or DAX), columns, relationships, hierarchies and column metadata through the powerbi-modeling MCP server. Does NOT write measures (pbi-dax), RLS roles (pbi-security) or report files (pbi-report), and never edits TMDL on disk while Desktop is open. - **pbi-researcher**: Lightweight Power BI / Fabric documentation verification subagent. Invoked by oh-my-pbi when deep verification of a new or uncertain topic is required. Searches official Microsoft Learn documentation and returns a structured result: verified status, source URL, and a 1-2 sentence accurate summary. Stateless — invoked per topic, returns one response. Never fixes anything — it routes each finding to the right agent. ([CPobs/PBI-assistance](https://github.com/CPobs/PBI-assistance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pbi-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **pbi-security**: Row-level security (RLS) specialist for the Power BI Desktop project currently open. À utiliser quand l'objectif est d'écrire un plugin, corriger une erreur, patcher le jeu ou comprendre une classe du code natif. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-bepinex.md))
- **per-aspera-bepinx-core**: Agent avancé pour le runtime patching complexe avec HarmonyX : transpilers, patches conditionnels, IL manipulation, interop IL2CPP bas-niveau. À utiliser quand per-aspera-bepinex ne suffit pas pour les cas complexes. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-bepinx-core.md))
- **per-aspera-debugging**: Agent de diagnostic BepInEx/IL2CPP pour Per Aspera. À utiliser quand vous avez une erreur, exception, crash, NullReferenceException, TypeLoadException, MissingMethodException, patch Harmony silencieux, ou des logs BepInEx à analyser. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-debugging.md))
- **per-aspera-general**: Agent de coordination générale pour Per Aspera. À utiliser pour les projets multi-domaines, créer un mod de A à Z, orchestrer plusieurs agents spécialisés, ou pour toute demande large qui ne rentre pas dans un seul domaine. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-general.md))
- **per-aspera-onboarding**: Guide d'onboarding pour nouveaux moddeurs Per Aspera. À utiliser quand vous débutez le modding, configurez l'environnement pour la première fois, créez votre premier mod, ou souhaitez comprendre quelle expertise utiliser pour quoi. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-onboarding.md))
- **per-aspera-sdk-coordinator**: Agent coordinateur pour l'écosystème SDK Per Aspera. Gère tous les besoins SDK : GameAPI, Climate, Events, Overrides, Wrappers, Commands, Core. Point d'entrée unique pour les requêtes SDK simples ou multi-composants. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-sdk-coordinator.md))
- **per-aspera-sdk-ui**: Agent spécialisé dans le développement UI uGUI pour les mods Per Aspera via le toolkit SDK (PerAspera.GameAPI.UI.Toolkit) : panneaux au look natif, barres/listes paginées, clonage de widgets natifs, intégration des données du jeu, gestion input. Charge /per-aspera-ui-toolkit. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-sdk-ui.md))
- **per-aspera-yaml**: Agent spécialisé dans la modification, l'analyse et la documentation du datamodel YAML de Per Aspera : buildings, resources, technologies, knowledge, categories, localisation. ([PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills](https://github.com/PerAsperaMods/per-aspera-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/per-aspera-modding/agents/per-aspera-yaml.md))

- **per-task-dollars-verifier**: Fresh-context adversarial verification of a single completed per-task-dollars task. Dispatch after the implementer reports success, with just the task id. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/per-task-dollars-verifier.md))

- **perc-architect**: Evaluates architecture options for a decision, with trade-offs, NFR impact, a light threat model and test implications, in ADR-ready form. - **perc-code-reviewer**: Reviews a phase's changed files for correctness, regressions, security, and test quality, and returns APPROVED, NEEDS_REVISION or FAILED with tiered issues; never edits. Use after each implementation phase, before the commit gate. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/src/packs/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-code-reviewer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-docs-writer**: Writes README sections, usage examples, API docs and release notes from the shipped change set. Use at closeout of a plan, or whenever user-facing behaviour or an API changed. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-docs-writer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-explorer**: Maps files, usages, patterns and entry points read-only and breadth-first, and returns a structured file list with findings. Use as the first discovery step before research, planning or implementation. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-explorer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-implementer**: Implements exactly one plan phase test-first with a minimal diff, runs the project's tests, linter and type-checker, and reports back with a proposed commit message; never commits. Use for each implementation phase of the plan, followed by the Code Review Subagent. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-implementer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-orchestrator**: Drives a plan, implement, review and commit lifecycle across specialist subagents, one small test-first phase at a time, with stop gates before each commit. Use for multi-phase feature or refactoring work that should be planned, reviewed and committed in reviewable steps. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/src/packs/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-orchestrator.md) · ★3)
- **perc-planner**: Writes a research-driven, test-first phased plan with NFRs, risks and acceptance criteria into the plan directory, ready for the Orchestrator Agent to execute. Use when a feature or refactoring needs a plan before any code is written. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-planner.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-qa-strategist**: Designs a risk-based test strategy for a change: test levels, coverage map, quality gates and flakiness mitigation. Use when the test approach for a phase or plan is non-trivial. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-qa-strategist.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-researcher**: Researches a subsystem in depth and returns its conventions, key files, patterns, prior art and risks as structured findings, without planning or coding. Use after exploration when a plan or phase needs deeper context. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/src/packs/plan-explore-review-commit/agents/perc-researcher.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **perc-security-auditor**: Reviews a change for security risk with a light threat model, OWASP mapping and concrete mitigations; never edits. - **perception-extractor**: The ONE vision pass of the analysis pipeline. LITERAL observation of ONE ad image into a standardized dataset — geometry+text (axes 1-2) AND scene+look (axes 3-4): text content + bbox + font scale + color + shadow; graphic placement + kind; canvas; medium; what-is-depicted (subjects/scene/framing) + literal light/finish facts; absence; per-axis confidence. OBSERVE-ONLY — records WHAT IS THERE / WHERE / HOW IT LOOKS, never what it MEANS. NO impression-naming (premium/honest/hero), NO register/mood, NO setting bucketing, NO real/fake judgement — those are the downstream text analysts. Use FIRST… ([mwroh-dev/marketing-ad-img](https://github.com/mwroh-dev/marketing-ad-img/blob/HEAD/agents/perception-extractor.md) · ★1)

- **perception-ml-engineer**: Automotive perception ML engineer developing machine learning models for vehicle environment understanding ([birol91/quorum-agents](https://github.com/birol91/quorum-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/automotive--perception-ml-engineer.md))
- **perception_agent**: 这是一位专业的内容感知和分析专家，擅长读取和理解用户输入的查询和文件内容，提取关键信息并结合用户需求进行综合分析。 ([myysophia/cli-agent](https://github.com/myysophia/cli-agent/blob/HEAD/.iflow/agents/perception-agent.md) · ★29 · 2 copies)
- **percy**: Senior performance engineer who reviews plans and diffs for performance and scalability — measurement-first, never speculation. Use when a plan or phase touches DB schema/query shapes, caching, pagination, hot-path endpoints, bundle-affecting frontend changes, or states a performance goal — and as the lead for performance/scaling audits. Findings must carry a measurement or a complexity argument tied to a named hot path; "this could be slow" is not a finding. - **Percy**: Use when: defining product vision, writing user stories, creating user journeys, breaking down features into requirements, prioritizing backlog, defining acceptance criteria, representing the customer perspective, or managing the requirements/ folder. - **perf**: Служебный агент Ревизора: скорость — CWV, цепочка LCP, вес, шрифты, сторонние скрипты, кэш и сжатие; вызывается командами /revizor:* ([zzinziver/revizor](https://github.com/zzinziver/revizor/blob/HEAD/plugins/revizor/agents/perf.md))
- **Perf Plan**: Planning & execution agent for Orleans.Lattice throughput/scaling investigations. Owns the perf issue tree (master + per-workload parent + phase children), drives the azure-throughput single-silo rig, and logs the safe throughput ceiling per workload before navigating the scaling space (WAL partitions, multi-account, multi-silo). ([NSTA1/Orleans.Lattice](https://github.com/NSTA1/Orleans.Lattice/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/perf-plan.agent.md) · ★3)
- **Perf Police**: Evidence-led performance review for mssql-python. Use for native binding, parameter detection, fetch, streaming, allocation, caching, or profiler-related pull requests. Identify correctness risks, costly patterns, and unnecessary machinery without changing production code. ([microsoft/mssql-python](https://github.com/microsoft/mssql-python/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/perf-police.agent.md) · ★483)
- **perf-3d**: Use after any change to src/components/three/ or src/lib/graphics.tsx. Guards the performance budget for budget Android phones on rural data. ([konankirajesh7-raj/village-verse](https://github.com/konankirajesh7-raj/village-verse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/perf-3d.md))
- **perf-a11y**: Performance and accessibility. Lighthouse ≥ 95 mobile, Core Web Vitals, WCAG 2.1 AA. ([Ashhadk7/Event-Planner-Expo](https://github.com/Ashhadk7/Event-Planner-Expo/blob/HEAD/Skills/agents/perf-a11y.md))
